# Size budgets in the 6.0 clean break

The written reason for every size budget 6.0 raised, and the budgets it lowered, as the bundle-budget rule asks (`.claude/rules/bundle-budget.md`). Sizes are gzip, measured by `scripts/check-size.mjs`. The PR that merges 6.0 into `main` pastes the `Budget-raise:` lines below into its body, which is what `check-size-ratchet.mjs` reads.

Budget-raise: site-layout 75.05 KB → 76.25 KB (measured 76.14 KB): 6.0 approved proposals merged in v6/prop (CodeBlock line diffs, Command ranked search and Kbd keys in the shell search).
Budget-raise: doc-layout 65 KB → 68.3 KB (measured 68.19 KB): 6.0 approved proposals merged in v6/prop (CodeBlock line diffs, Command ranked search and Kbd keys in the docs search).
Budget-raise: doc-markdown 23.85 KB → 24.7 KB (measured 24.57 KB): 6.0 DocTooltip folded into HoverCard, which DocMarkdown renders for the MDX Tooltip tag (v6/docs), and 6.0 B11 (components follow the device locale, read on mount).
Budget-raise: search-trigger 9 KB → 9.35 KB (measured 9.21 KB): 6.0 approved proposals merged in v6/prop (Kbd keys list).
Budget-raise: password-requirements 3.7 KB → 3.9 KB (measured 3.78 KB): 6.0 B11 (components follow the device locale, read on mount).

Budget-raise: quote-tabs 23.85 KB → 24.05 KB (measured 23.91 KB): A15 text wordmarks for brands whose terms refuse their logo, in the brand-mark registry (owner 2026-11-17).

## What an `entryOnly` budget leaves out (2026-11-17, M2.10 row 82)

`node scripts/check-size.mjs --lazy <entry>` lists, under each `entryOnly` entry, every chunk its initial code can request on render, with the gzipped size of what that request loads beyond the entry. For `media-split-section` (14.46 KB counted) and `media-split-stack-section` (14.79 KB counted) the excluded chunks are the scenes (`console-application-scene` 124.12 KB, `console-trace-scene` 71.53 KB, `brand-globe-scene` 15.13 KB, `diagram` 14.24 KB, `spinning-globe` 6.57 KB, `runtime-api-scene` 6.21 KB, `runtime-api-cloud` 5.44 KB), the console mockup (2.46 KB) and the illustration runtime (`registry` 1.56 KB, `scene` 1.32 KB). The scenes and the mockup load only when the page data names one and carry their own budgets, so leaving them out is right. The illustration runtime is the one real under-count: a section with an `illustration` loads 2.88 KB plus its drawing on top of the counted entry. The budgets stay as they are; a section page that always draws an illustration should read the `--lazy` line next to the entry.
Budget-raise: phone-input 29.1 KB → 33.25 KB (measured 33.13 KB): owner request 2026-11-18, PhoneInput offers every country and territory with a calling code (245, from libphonenumber metadata) instead of a curated 34, and the closed trigger shows the flag, name and dial code.
Budget-raise: hero-form-section 45.85 KB → 50.6 KB (measured 50.47 KB): its phone field is PhoneInput, which now offers every country and territory with a calling code (owner request 2026-11-18).
Budget-raise: job-posting-section 51.5 KB → 56.25 KB (measured 56.15 KB): its phone field is PhoneInput, which now offers every country and territory with a calling code (owner request 2026-11-18).
Budget-raise: listing-index-section 39.85 KB → 39.95 KB (measured 39.85 KB): Select's trigger gained the trailing slot PhoneInput uses for the dial code (owner request 2026-11-18), on a budget with no headroom.
Budget-raise: log-viewer-content 28.4 KB → 28.5 KB (measured 28.45 KB): owner request 2026-11-18, the time and level are columns of the whole log (each sized to its longest value), so every message starts at the same x; the search highlight was folded into one render helper to pay for most of it, on a budget with 0.01 KB headroom.
Budget-raise: log-viewer 42.6 KB → 42.7 KB (measured 42.65 KB): the LogViewer.Content log columns above.

Budget-raise: console-trace-scene 73.9 KB → 74.05 KB (measured 73.97 KB): renders LogViewer.Content, the log columns above (+0.02 KB); it already measured 73.95 KB, over its budget, before this change.
Budget-raise: console-application-scene 115.8 KB → 115.95 KB (measured 115.9 KB): owner report 2026-11-18, Tabs.List placed its active indicator in on-screen pixels, so inside a CSS-scaled console mock-up the highlight sat off its label; it now measures in the list's own layout pixels and re-measures when any tab changes size (+0.12 KB on every Tabs.List, which is also the 73.95 KB console-trace-scene measured above).
Budget-raise: table-cell-time 18.7 KB → 18.75 KB (measured 18.70 KB): it renders Avatar, whose default pattern colors moved off the removed --text-primary to the logo orange and brand blue (owner decision RV-H1, 2026-11-18; +a few bytes).
Budget-raise: doc-markdown 26 KB → 26.05 KB (measured 26.00 KB, a few bytes over): owner report 2026-11-18, the inline code chip of DocProse, DocCallout and DocItem also matched `pre > code` and drew one pill per line of a code block; the chip selectors now exclude code inside `pre`.
Budget-raise: doc-callout 6.3 KB → 8.45 KB (measured 8.36 KB): owner request 2026-11-18, an inline command in a callout copies on click like DocProse chips do, and a chip marked data-command is a keyboard button with a polite Copied announcement; the callout now carries the shared useInlineCodeCopy composable, the clipboard helper, the copy tooltip and the copy messages.
Budget-raise: doc-prose 3.75 KB → 4.35 KB (measured 4.28 KB): the inline-code copy moved into the shared useInlineCodeCopy composable and gained the keyboard path for command chips (role, tabindex, accessible name, Enter and Space) and a polite live region.
Budget-raise: doc-markdown 26.05 KB → 28.1 KB (measured with both 2026-11-18 changes): renders DocCallout, which now copies its inline commands (the doc-callout raise above).
Budget-raise: article-body-section 45.1 KB → 45.75 KB (measured 45.62 KB): renders DocProse, whose inline-code copy gained the keyboard path for command chips and a polite live region (the doc-prose raise above).
Budget-raise: compliance-logos-section 7.9 KB → 7.95 KB (measured 7.91 KB): owner report 2026-11-18 (RV05, RV-C1), MediaSplit splits on its own width (a container query) instead of the window's and pads a large copy one step tighter in a narrow split, so TemplateGallerySection keeps its 50/50 split with a two-line headline; the band renders MediaSplit inline, on a budget with 0 KB headroom.
Budget-raise: tutorial-panel 9.55 KB → 9.75 KB (measured 9.72 KB): the hotspot's five locale strings moved into the shared `tutorialPanel` group when `Hotspot` became `TutorialPanelHotspot`, and the provider keeps one open tutorial step per page (owner report 2026-11-18, two step popovers opened at once on the Hotspot page).
Budget-raise: tutorial-panel-root 8.9 KB → 9 KB (measured 8.94 KB): the same five locale strings, now in the `tutorialPanel` group the panel reads.
Budget-raise: tutorial-panel-provider 1.1 KB → 1.15 KB (measured 1.13 KB): the one-open-step owner (`lib/open-step-owner.ts`), so starting a tutorial in one provider closes the step another provider had open.
Budget-raise: tutorial-panel-hotspot 13 KB → 13.25 KB (measured 13.21 KB): renamed from `hotspot` (13 KB); it now reads the whole `tutorialPanel` locale group instead of its own five strings.
Budget-raise: locales-pt-BR 10.5 KB → 10.55 KB (measured 10.50 KB): merging the website naming review with the owner review fixes of 2026-11-18 puts the `docTableOfContents` group (renamed from `docOnThisPage`) and the hotspot strings moved into `tutorialPanel` in one catalog, past a budget with no headroom.
Budget-raise: client-quotes-section 26.6 KB → 26.7 KB (measured 26.63 KB): WN-S02 moved the page frame (`container`, `spacer`, `module`, `module-header`) from `marketing/` to `layout/`; the import sort that follows reorders the bundled modules, which gzips slightly worse. No new code.
Budget-raise: photo-marquee-section 7.25 KB → 7.3 KB (measured 7.25 KB): WN-S02 moved the page frame (`container`, `spacer`, `module`, `module-header`) from `marketing/` to `layout/`; the import sort that follows reorders the bundled modules, which gzips slightly worse. No new code.
Budget-raise: template-gallery-section 28.45 KB → 28.65 KB (measured 28.58 KB): WN-S02 moved the page frame (`container`, `spacer`, `module`, `module-header`) from `marketing/` to `layout/`; the import sort that follows reorders the bundled modules, which gzips slightly worse. No new code.
Budget-raise: recognition-marquee-section 21.15 KB → 21.35 KB (measured 21.31 KB): WN-S02 moved the page frame (`container`, `spacer`, `module`, `module-header`) from `marketing/` to `layout/`; the import sort that follows reorders the bundled modules, which gzips slightly worse. No new code.
Budget-raise: tree 10.1 KB → 10.35 KB (measured 10.28 KB): owner report 2026-11-18 (RV-T1, RV-T2), the new `size` prop (small, medium, large rows with their padding, type and indent steps), the inset 1px focus border and the guard that keeps Shift and Ctrl+click from selecting label text.
Budget-raise: doc-markdown 28.1 KB → 28.25 KB (measured 28.10 KB): RV-M1, DocTabs and DocTab are removed and DocMarkdown renders the MDX `<Tabs><Tab title>` tags straight onto the four `Tabs` parts (four lazy imports and the panel mapping instead of two lazy DocTabs imports).
Budget-raise: locales-es 10.8 KB → 10.85 KB (measured 10.80 KB): merging the website naming review with the owner review fixes of 2026-11-18 puts the renamed `docTableOfContents` group and the new date picker and tree messages in one catalog.
Budget-raise: css-button 10.85 KB → 11.1 KB (measured 11.04 KB; the base branch already measured 10.94 KB, over its limit): owner report 2026-11-18 (RV-B1), the theme's three medium-weight `text-title-*` roles add their custom properties (size, line height, weight, tracking, wrap, and the two breakpoint sizes of `text-title-lg`) to every app's stylesheet.
Budget-raise: css-console 24 KB → 24.1 KB (measured 24.01 KB): the same `text-title-*` role properties (RV-B1).
Budget-raise: recognition-marquee-section 21.15 KB → 21.2 KB (measured 21.16 KB): `cn` now knows the `title`, `amount` and `tag` type roles, so it no longer drops them when a text colour follows (RV-B1).
Budget-raise: code-block 32.4 KB → 32.45 KB (measured 32.4 KB): the same `cn` role list (RV-B1).
Budget-raise: schema-form 61 KB → 61.05 KB: the same `cn` role list (RV-B1), on top of the date range picker raise.
Budget-raise: date-picker 24.6 KB → 41.7 KB (measured 41.57 KB): the calendar caption gains Month and Year selects between its arrows (owner 2026-11-18, decision RV-S2), built on the WebKit `Select` because the owner wants no native select; the Select's list brings `ScrollArea` and with it `cn` (tailwind-merge), which the date picker did not carry before.
Budget-raise: date-picker-root 24.4 KB → 41.5 KB (measured 41.36 KB): the same Month and Year `Select`s in the shared calendar caption (2026-11-18).
Budget-raise: date-range-picker 44.85 KB → 51.05 KB (measured 50.91 KB): the same Month and Year `Select`s, plus the Unit and Refresh every selects and the time zone selector moved from native selects to the WebKit `Select` and `Combobox` (owner 2026-11-18, decision RV-S4).
Budget-raise: locales-es 10.8 KB → 10.85 KB (measured 10.80 KB): the Month and Year names of the calendar caption selects (2026-11-18).
Budget-raise: flow-diagram-root 13.5 KB → 13.75 KB (measured 13.52 KB): the `uniform` prop (equal-size steps) the edge error page's request path needs (owner decision RV-D1, 2026-11-18).
Budget-raise: stats-section 4.5 KB → 4.65 KB (measured 4.63 KB): owner report 2026-11-18 (RV-U1), a Stats figure never wraps; the band counts the glyphs of its longest figure so the big-number role shrinks every figure to fit its cell.
Budget-raise: network-section 26.65 KB → 26.8 KB (measured 26.79 KB): the same Stats figure fit, which the network band renders (RV-U1).
Budget-raise: topology-diagram 27.95 KB → 28.1 KB (measured 28.03 KB): the `orientation` prop (`vertical` stacks the levels with a dashed line between them), so the edge error page draws its request path with TopologyDiagram and stacks it on a phone (owner decision RV-Z2, 2026-11-18).
Budget-raise: flow-diagram 14.5 KB → 21.35 KB (measured 21.27 KB): owner decision RV-Y1 (2026-11-18) merges TopologyDiagram into FlowDiagram; the compound gains the Header, Title, Description, Footer and Item parts and the data mode (`nodes` + `edges`: column ranking, labelled and coloured edges, the hidden connections list, the locale strings). The data card, the empty state and the loading skeleton are lazy chunks, so a composed diagram never downloads them; TopologyDiagram's own 28.1 KB entry is removed.
Budget-raise: flow-diagram-root 13.75 KB → 19.3 KB (measured 19.2 KB): the same data mode on the standalone root (RV-Y1, 2026-11-18).
Budget-raise: console-trace-scene 74.05 KB → 82 KB (measured 81.91 KB): the scene draws its path with the unified FlowDiagram (RV-Y1, 2026-11-18); the entry counts the diagram's lazy data card (copy button) and its locale strings although the scene composes its nodes by hand.
Budget-raise: hero-section 12 KB → 12.45 KB (measured 12.35 KB): the hero topology renders the unified FlowDiagram root (RV-Y1, 2026-11-18), which carries the data mode and the locale strings.
Budget-raise: date-range-picker 51.05 KB → 52.05 KB (measured 51.92 KB): the date limits the owner asked for (default `min` five years back and `max` the end of today, the new `maxDuration` applied to the calendar, the quick ranges, typed ranges and custom durations, with its message) and the Custom range row that opens the duration form on demand with Back to quick ranges (owner 2026-11-18, decisions RV-D2b and RV-D2c); removing the zone line saved part of it.

Budget-raise: doc-markdown 28.25 KB → 28.8 KB (measured 28.70 KB): RV-F2-1, content channels; braced attribute values are read as JSON (a balanced-brace scanner and a relaxed JSON fallback), so `<FlowDiagram nodes={[…]} edges={[…]} />` carries the same data as the props and the page JSON. Budget-raise: date-picker-root 43.15 KB → 43.65 KB (measured 43.60 KB): DatePicker and DateRangePicker now build their trigger control, trigger button, control buttons and panel from four shared private parts in date-picker/parts (the look-alike pair date-picker::date-range-picker, decision CQ-E); each part is its own small component, which costs a few hundred bytes of component wiring in each entry. Dropping cn (tailwind-merge) from the parts kept the raise to this.

Budget-raise: card-carousel-section 7.65 KB → 9.3 KB (measured 9.27 KB): the carousel controls it renders are IconButton instead of hand-written buttons (no-unowned-element).
Budget-raise: tool-approval 14.8 KB → 15.8 KB (measured 15.77 KB): the confirm phrase field is Input instead of a hand-written input (no-unowned-element).

Budget-raise: link 2.35 KB → 2.45 KB (measured 2.40 KB): the new `inline` mode, a second anchor branch for links inside a sentence, so blocks stop writing raw `<a class="text-link">`.

Budget-raise: transfer-list 6.75 KB → 7.9 KB (measured 7.85 KB): it renders TransferList.Controls, which now builds on IconButton.

Budget-raise: popover 11.2 KB → 11.3 KB (measured 11.29 KB, base 11.18 KB): it bundles usePlacement (+127 B), the listbox, select-search, lazy-chunks, inline-copy and button-root composables (+11 to +141 B each); the bytes are the function boundaries of the CODE_QUALITY 3.1 split (step 6, no baseline), after the shrink pass removed the option and return objects.
Budget-raise: popover-root 5.15 KB → 5.25 KB (measured 5.21 KB, base 5.07 KB): it bundles usePlacement (+127 B); the bytes are the function boundaries of the CODE_QUALITY 3.1 split (step 6, no baseline), after the shrink pass removed the option and return objects.
Budget-raise: property-filter 34.55 KB → 36.4 KB (measured 36.36 KB): CODE_QUALITY 3.1 split of the 947-line root into six composables, two private parts and lib modules; the option and return keys between them and the two part components are not mangled by the minifier.
Budget-raise: tree 10.35 KB → 10.9 KB (measured 10.85 KB): CODE_QUALITY 3.1 split of the 600-line root into five composables and lib modules; the option and return keys between them are not mangled by the minifier.
Budget-raise: data-grid 11.55 KB → 13.25 KB (measured 13.22 KB): CODE_QUALITY 3.1 split of the 844-line root into six composables, two private parts and lib modules; the option and return keys between them and the two part components are not mangled by the minifier.
Budget-raise: data-table 58.05 KB → 58.5 KB (measured 58.46 KB): CODE_QUALITY 3.1 first split of the 2,156-line root (filter matchers as a table, row heights and row focus composables, lib helpers); the host and return keys between them are not mangled by the minifier.
Budget-raise: navigation-menu 26.1 KB → 26.55 KB (measured 26.52 KB): split of the root, positioner and highlight composables into sub-60-line functions (CODE_QUALITY 3.1); esbuild cannot mangle the context keys the split functions share.
Budget-raise: navigation-menu-root 15.55 KB → 15.8 KB (measured 15.76 KB): same split: the 290-line root composable becomes trigger registry, element slots, value state, popup state, timers and a keyboard composable that share their state through object keys.
Budget-raise: navigation-menu-viewport 18.35 KB → 18.4 KB (measured 18.38 KB): carries the navigation menu root composable split.
Budget-raise: sidebar-menu 21.95 KB → 22 KB (measured 21.98 KB): drill levels moved to use-sidebar-menu-levels to bring the script under 200 lines; the levels object is spread into the context.
Budget-raise: sidebar-menu-root 21.5 KB → 21.6 KB (measured 21.55 KB): same levels composable split as sidebar-menu.
Budget-raise: console-layout-root 81.6 KB → 81.7 KB (measured 81.66 KB): carries the sidebar menu and navigation menu splits.
Budget-raise: console-layout 82.95 KB → 83 KB (measured 82.97 KB): carries the sidebar menu and navigation menu splits.
Budget-raise: doc-layout 72.15 KB → 72.4 KB (measured 72.36 KB): carries the navigation menu, sidebar menu and command splits and the outline composable.
Budget-raise: site-header-section 58.85 KB → 59.35 KB (measured 59.32 KB): carries the navigation menu composable split.
Budget-raise: job-posting-section 58.6 KB → 58.65 KB (measured 58.61 KB): form state moved to use-job-posting-form and the rail to a part to bring the section under 400 lines.
Budget-raise: diagram 11.65 KB → 11.7 KB (measured 11.65 KB): connector layout moved out of the measure callback into module functions (function limit 60).
Budget-raise: button-group 16.7 KB → 16.75 KB (measured 16.70 KB): carries the popover and trigger-host keydown and attribute splits (complexity 12).
Budget-raise: flow-diagram-bind 16 KB → 16.05 KB (measured 16.01 KB): carries the popover keydown split (complexity 12).
Budget-raise: doc-markdown 40 KB → 40.2 KB (measured 40.15 KB): the renderer and the block parser move to lib/ (script 200, module 300, function 60 lines); the tag registry stays per instance so a resolved async tag never changes the hydration order, and the render functions read it through a context object esbuild cannot shorten.
Budget-raise: command-root 21.55 KB → 21.6 KB (measured 21.57 KB): the base was already over at 21.56 KB; pages and roving move to use-command-pages and use-command-active, and the active value is returned read-only with a setter (composables rule).
Budget-raise: doc-code-group 33.25 KB → 34.1 KB (measured 34.09 KB): it renders CodeBlock, whose tabs, lines and motion moved to parts and a composable to meet the 400/200-line limits (CODE_QUALITY 3.1).
Budget-raise: console-application-scene 119.9 KB → 120.6 KB (measured 120.58 KB): it renders CodeBlock and Chart, split into parts and composables to meet the 400/200-line limits (CODE_QUALITY 3.1).
Budget-raise: console-trace-scene 83.85 KB → 84.5 KB (measured 84.45 KB): it renders Trace and CodeBlock, split into parts and composables to meet the 400/200-line limits (CODE_QUALITY 3.1).
Budget-raise: cli-terminal-section 46.5 KB → 47 KB (measured 46.98 KB): it renders CodeBlock, split into parts and a composable to meet the 400/200-line limits (CODE_QUALITY 3.1).
Budget-raise: feature-tabs-section 57.4 KB → 58 KB (measured 57.98 KB): it renders CodeBlock, split into parts and a composable to meet the 400/200-line limits (CODE_QUALITY 3.1).
Budget-raise: chart 26.3 KB → 27 KB (measured 26.95 KB): the Chart root (528 script lines) split into series, scales, readout, interaction, pointer and keyboard composables to meet the 200-line script and 60-line function limits (CODE_QUALITY 3.1); each module adds its option object and return object.
Budget-raise: chart-root 17.5 KB → 18.15 KB (measured 18.15 KB): the same Chart split, measured on the standalone root.
Budget-raise: code-block 32.8 KB → 33.7 KB (measured 33.66 KB): the CodeBlock root (858 lines) split into the tabs and lines parts, the lines and motion composables and one grammar file per language to meet the 400/200-line, 60-line and complexity-12 limits (CODE_QUALITY 3.1); each part adds a component definition and its props.
Budget-raise: trace 37.5 KB → 38.9 KB (measured 38.85 KB): the Trace root (1,274 lines) split into the minimap, timeline, rows and detail parts, the detail and navigation composables and the model lib to meet the 400/200-line limits (CODE_QUALITY 3.1); each part adds a component definition and its props.
Budget-raise: tool-approval 15.8 KB → 15.95 KB (measured 15.94 KB): it renders the AI markdown parser and inline renderer, split into one scanner per syntax to meet the 60-line and complexity-12 limits (CODE_QUALITY 3.1).
Budget-raise: agent-question 16.6 KB → 16.75 KB (measured 16.71 KB): the same AI markdown parser and inline renderer split.
Budget-raise: prompt-input 13.1 KB → 13.85 KB (measured 13.84 KB): the PromptInput root (612 lines) split into the menu list part and the menu, submit, attach and autosize composables to meet the 400/200-line limits (CODE_QUALITY 3.1).
Budget-raise: prompt-input-root 10 KB → 10.7 KB (measured 10.69 KB): the same PromptInput split, measured on the standalone root.
Budget-raise: streaming-response 36.85 KB → 37.95 KB (measured 37.92 KB): it renders CodeBlock and the AI markdown parser, both split to meet the CODE_QUALITY 3.1 limits.
Budget-raise: code-editor 10.6 KB → 11.5 KB (measured 11.49 KB): the CodeEditor root (737 lines) split into the pane and problems parts, the Monaco composable and session lib to meet the 400/200-line, 60-line and complexity-12 limits (CODE_QUALITY 3.1).
Budget-raise: app-layout 24.1 KB → 24.4 KB (measured 24.37 KB): CODE_QUALITY 3.1/3.3 split: the split panel, written twice (bottom and side), renders once from a private part, and the localStorage sync moved to a composable; the part boundary costs more than the duplicate template, which gzip had nearly for free.
Budget-raise: console-layout-root 81.6 KB → 82.45 KB (measured 82.40 KB): carries the Sidebar and AppLayout splits (0.45 KB), plus the 3.1 split of ConsoleLayout (palette and header-start parts, nav audit composable, reveal helper) to bring the root under 400 lines.
Budget-raise: console-layout 82.95 KB → 83.7 KB (measured 83.68 KB): same as console-layout-root.
Budget-raise: doc-layout 72.15 KB → 72.4 KB (measured 72.38 KB): carries the Sidebar and AppLayout splits.
Budget-raise: sidebar 24.95 KB → 25.1 KB (measured 25.08 KB): CODE_QUALITY 3.1 split of useSidebarRail (164-line function) into drag, preview and key composables; the shared refs passed between them and the extra return objects cost the bytes, and splitting the Sidebar setup below 200 lines moved code out of the gzip window of its template.
Budget-raise: sidebar-root 24.75 KB → 24.9 KB (measured 24.88 KB): same split of useSidebarRail and the Sidebar setup as the sidebar entry.
Budget-raise: combobox 13.55 KB → 13.85 KB (measured 13.80 KB): split into rows, commit, scroll and keyboard composables for the CODE_QUALITY 3.1 limits; the function boundaries and the names they return cost 0.28 KB that inlining would have saved.
Budget-raise: date-picker-root 43.7 KB → 43.8 KB (measured 43.78 KB): the date picker fields and time zone render Combobox, which grew 0.28 KB in its split.
Budget-raise: date-picker 43.9 KB → 43.95 KB (measured 43.93 KB): the date picker fields and time zone render Combobox, which grew 0.28 KB in its split.
Budget-raise: attribute-editor 18.85 KB → 20.45 KB (measured 20.42 KB, base 18.75 KB): split for the CODE_QUALITY 3.1 limits (CQ-E) into one rows composable, one drag composable, lib/rows.ts and two private parts (group bar, row); the call boundaries and the parts' context cost 1.67 KB.
Budget-raise: tag-editor 20.8 KB → 22.25 KB (measured 22.21 KB, base 20.64 KB): renders AttributeEditor, which grew 1.67 KB in its split for the CODE_QUALITY 3.1 limits.
Budget-raise: schema-form 62.55 KB → 63.45 KB (measured 63.40 KB, base 61.33 KB): split for the CODE_QUALITY 3.1 limits into parts, composables and lib modules (+0.22 KB), and it renders Combobox (+0.28 KB) and AttributeEditor (+1.57 KB), both split for the same limits.
Budget-raise: date-range-picker 54.7 KB → 56.15 KB (measured 56.10 KB, base 54.34 KB): split for the CODE_QUALITY 3.1 limits (CQ-E) into lib modules (labels, bounds, draft, panel actions), five composables (clock and refresh, open state, grid context, draft, trigger and step) and two private parts with props and events (relative panel, footer) (+1.54 KB), and it renders Combobox in the date fields (+0.22 KB).
Budget-raise: package packedBytes 8400000 → 8500000 (measured 8465155): the CODE_QUALITY 3.1 splits of components and blocks into parts/, lib/ and composables/ add hundreds of small files, and each costs a tar header and its own compression window in the packed tarball.
Budget-raise: table-search 5.1 KB → 5.15 KB (measured 5.12 KB): it now holds the search text as its v-model (moved off DataTable, CODE_QUALITY 3.4) and syncs it with the table both ways.
Budget-raise: table-filter 6.5 KB → 6.65 KB (measured 6.64 KB): it now holds the structured filters as its v-model and registers its fields with the table (both moved off DataTable, CODE_QUALITY 3.4).
Budget-raise: table-export-button 8.95 KB → 9 KB (measured 8.97 KB): it now takes the export rows (moved off DataTable's exportRows, CODE_QUALITY 3.4) and awaits them before the export.
Budget-raise: table-live-button 4.45 KB → 4.6 KB (measured 4.55 KB): it now holds the live tail as its v-model (moved off DataTable, CODE_QUALITY 3.4) and syncs it with the table both ways.

## Audit-5 merge (2026-11-23)

Several audit-5 lanes raised the same entry for their own fix; merged together, the entry carries all of those changes. Each limit below is the measured size of the merged branch rounded up to the next 0.05 KB. The AI bundle budgets also rose: `llms-full.txt` 2,360,000 → 2,400,000 bytes (measured 2,374,456) and `llms-full-site.txt` 750,000 → 760,000 bytes (measured 751,746), from the specs and guidelines the lanes extended.

Budget-raise: prompt-input-root 10.75 KB → 11.35 KB (measured 11.32 KB): the single floating pill that stacks into a rounded rectangle on a second line (the multiline state, the send part split out of the root to keep it under the size limits, the translucent blurred surface).
Budget-raise: prompt-input 13.9 KB → 14.5 KB (measured 14.44 KB): the same floating pill as prompt-input-root, plus round Attach and Dictate controls.
Budget-raise: prompt-input-context-picker 15.2 KB → 15.25 KB (measured 15.20 KB): its trigger is now round to sit in the composer pill.
Budget-raise: prompt-queue 4.7 KB → 4.75 KB (measured 4.71 KB): the queue box takes the composer's translucent surface and backdrop blur (chat look proposal), so it reads as one floating surface with the pill composer below it.
Budget-raise: conversation-history 6.7 KB → 9 KB (measured 8.94 KB): the ChatGPT-style side list adds New chat and a search field (`creatable`, `searchable`); the search field is the package `Input` (frame, field attributes, autofill hints), which a native input may not replace (owners.json).

## Audit-5 follow-ups, after the merge (2026-11-23)

Every entry below was within its budget on 30d1ab447; the follow-up lanes (and the splits that bring command.vue and date-picker.vue under the CODE_QUALITY 3.1 script limit) grow each a little. Each line names the changes the entry statically imports.

Budget-raise: console-layout-root 83.4 KB → 84.15 KB (measured 84.10 KB, within budget on 30d1ab447): Command moves ranked results into the ranked DOM order (A5-299), and its ordering logic moves into useCommandOrder to meet the CODE_QUALITY 3.1 script limit; the Tooltip it carries stays closed after Escape until the pointer leaves the trigger (A5-FU-B1-2); numeric Tag, Badge and +N labels stay left to right in rtl (labelDirection) (merge of the audit-5 follow-up lanes, A5-FU-POST-1).

## Page sections on the shared SectionBand frame (2026-11-23, roadmap 1.7)

Thirty-one page sections now draw their outer frame through the private `SectionBand` part (and `SectionHatch`, `SectionActions`) instead of a copy each. Measured alone, each entry carries the part; a page loads it once, and all 46 sections bundled together shrink from 1,615.95 KB to 1,615.56 KB.

## StatTile loading keeps the loaded shape (2026-11-24, audit-5 P3, A5P3-138)

While `loading`, StatTile now reserves the delta line (when `deltaLabel` is set) and the sparkline (when `trend` has two or more points) with Skeletons, as its spec already promised, so a row of KPI tiles no longer grows by about 80 px when the data lands.

Budget-raise: stat-tile 9.35 KB → 9.4 KB (measured 9.39 KB): two loading Skeletons that reserve the delta line and the sparkline height (A5P3-138), so dashboards do not shift on arrival.
Budget-raise: context-menu 11.7 KB → 11.75 KB (measured 11.71 KB): its items gain the danger severity for destructive actions such as Delete (A5P3-066, A5P3-L04-2); the shared item row carries one more prop and one class.

## One reason per raised entry (2026-11-24, audit-5 P3 lane L13)

`check-size-ratchet.mjs` now asks for one `Budget-raise: <name> …: <reason>` line per raised entry, read from the PR body and from this file, instead of any single `Budget-raise:` line. Against `main` one raise had no line of its own:

Budget-raise: network-map 6.5 KB → 7.1 KB (measured 6.97 KB): NetworkMap traffic (MapMesh rays and packets, a still frame under reduced motion), on in BannerArt network (M2.10 row 43); it peaked at 7.5 KB and was lowered to 7.1 KB.

FlowDiagram's fit stops probing a row layout on every resize callback (A5P3-242): it remembers the scroller and list size it last probed at and skips the probe when they have not changed, and a step change cancels the pending frame instead of fitting twice. Measured on a height-only resize of the viewport, the row probes (two style and layout passes each) dropped from 2 to 0.

Budget-raise: flow-diagram 22 KB → 22.1 KB (measured 22.05 KB): FlowDiagram fit skips the row probe when the scroller and list size are unchanged (audit-5 P3 A5P3-242, resize-only layout work halved).
Budget-raise: flow-diagram-root 19.75 KB → 19.85 KB (measured 19.79 KB): FlowDiagram fit skips the row probe when the scroller and list size are unchanged (audit-5 P3 A5P3-242).
Budget-raise: console-trace-scene 85.5 KB → 85.6 KB (measured 85.56 KB): carries FlowDiagram, whose fit skips the row probe when the scroller and list size are unchanged (audit-5 P3 A5P3-242).

The autoplay hairline of QuoteTabs, MediaTabs and ArticleHighlightSection (A5P3-235) no longer re-renders ten times a second: one `setTimeout` per slide advances the band, the hairline is one catalogued `animate-autoplay-progress` (a `scale`, not a `width`) restarted per slide and held with `animation-play-state`, and an IntersectionObserver holds the rotation while the band is off screen. Before: 10 reactive updates and 10 style recalculations of the bar per second, off screen too; after: none between slides, none off screen.

Budget-raise: media-tabs 10.25 KB → 10.45 KB (measured 10.41 KB): MediaTabs autoplay advances on one timeout per slide with a CSS hairline and holds off screen (audit-5 P3 A5P3-235).
Budget-raise: quote-tabs 24.6 KB → 24.85 KB (measured 24.78 KB): QuoteTabs autoplay advances on one timeout per slide with a CSS hairline and holds off screen (audit-5 P3 A5P3-235).
Budget-raise: article-highlight-section 12.65 KB → 13.05 KB (measured 12.98 KB): ArticleHighlightSection, now on the shared useAutoplayProgress instead of its own copy, autoplay advances on one timeout per slide with a CSS hairline and holds off screen (audit-5 P3 A5P3-235).
Budget-raise: client-quotes-section 27.85 KB → 28.05 KB (measured 28.00 KB): carries QuoteTabs, whose autoplay advances on one timeout per slide with a CSS hairline and holds off screen (audit-5 P3 A5P3-235).
Budget-raise: feature-tabs-section 59.9 KB → 60.1 KB (measured 60.06 KB): carries MediaTabs, whose autoplay advances on one timeout per slide with a CSS hairline and holds off screen (audit-5 P3 A5P3-235).

## Copilot projects (2026-11-23)

Budget-raise: conversation-history 9.05 KB → 11.85 KB (measured 11.81 KB): the ChatGPT-style Projects section (owner request 2026-11-23): `projects`, `projectCreatable`, `v-model:openKeys`, `v-model:projectsOpen` and `v-model:projectSort`, a collapsible Projects header with New project, folder rows that expand in place, a Recents label, search across projects and eight new locale strings; the row moves to a private part shared by project and Recents lists, and grouping, project filing and focus-after-delete move to three composables (CODE_QUALITY.md 3.1). The Organize menu (`DropdownMenu`, 11.28 KB) loads as a lazy chunk, so the budget becomes `entryOnly`.
Budget-raise: config-provider 12.8 KB → 12.85 KB (measured 12.81 KB): the English catalog it bundles gains the ConversationHistory project strings (Projects, Recents, New project, Organize projects, sort labels, empty project), owner 2026-11-23.
Budget-raise: locales-pt-BR 12 KB → 12.05 KB (measured 12.01 KB): the ConversationHistory project strings in Portuguese, owner 2026-11-23.
Budget-raise: locales-es 12.1 KB → 12.2 KB (measured 12.15 KB): the ConversationHistory project strings in Spanish, owner 2026-11-23.
Budget-raise: conversation-history 11.85 KB → 12.1 KB (measured 12.06 KB): every row moves to the SidebarMenuItem menu row (owner 2026-11-23): 32 px rows (44 px on touch) with the inset fill and no list gap, a 32 px icon box on New chat and the project rows, the SidebarMenuGroup label on Recents and the date groups, and a hover title that shows only on a cut-off title (a small private helper that measures the label).

## Sources list on DropdownMenu (2026-11-23)

The owner asked for the Sources list to be built from an existing component instead of an ad hoc popover list. It is now a `DropdownMenu` of link items, and `DropdownMenu.Item` gains `target` (`_blank`: `rel`, the external-link glyph and the translated `common.newTab` hint, which brings `useTranslate` into the item) and `description` (a second line named by `aria-describedby`). Every entry that bundles the item grows with it.

Budget-raise: sources 11.8 KB → 14.45 KB (measured 14.38 KB): the source list is a `DropdownMenu` (trigger, groups, link items, roving focus and typeahead) instead of a `Popover` with a hand-made list, owner 2026-11-23.

Budget-raise: console-layout-root 85.4 KB → 85.6 KB (measured 85.49 KB): the teleport hydration fix (port of #1210): a body teleport stays inline on the server and while hydrating, then moves to the body once mounted, so a page hydrated without Vue's teleport buffer (Astro) can open a second overlay; the shared useTeleportReady composable adds one ref, a computed and an onMounted per overlay.

## Raises after audit 6

From here on a raise names what grew. The size ratchet fails a line below this heading (or in the PR body) whose only reason is a merge, such as "each side fit its own budget" (`MERGE_ONLY_FROM` in `scripts/check-size-ratchet.mjs`, audit-6 A6-288).

Budget-raise: conversation-history 42.35 KB → 17.9 KB entryOnly (measured 17.74 KB): restores entryOnly, which the 17.25 KB raise intended: the row, project, sort and toolbar menus load as lazy chunks (52.9 KB together, listed by `check-size --lazy`), so the budget again measures the entry and its static imports. A lower limit, not a raise (audit-6 A6-271).

Budget-raise: app-layout 24.55 KB → 24.6 KB (measured 24.56 KB): IconButton keeps a transparent fill while a ghost button is disabled (A6-232); every entry that renders IconButton carries the few extra bytes.

Budget-raise: collection-preferences 32.95 KB → 33 KB (measured 32.95 KB): IconButton keeps a transparent fill while a ghost button is disabled (A6-232); every entry that renders IconButton carries the few extra bytes.

Budget-raise: site-header-section 60.2 KB → 60.25 KB (measured 60.22 KB): IconButton keeps a transparent fill while a ghost button is disabled (A6-232); every entry that renders IconButton carries the few extra bytes.

Budget-raise: chart 27.55 KB → 27.6 KB (measured 27.55 KB): IconButton keeps a transparent fill while a ghost button is disabled (A6-232); every entry that renders IconButton carries the few extra bytes.

Budget-raise: error-boundary 7.3 KB → 7.35 KB (measured 7.32 KB): ErrorBoundary moves focus to a custom fallback when the failure leaves focus on the body or a disabled control (A6-006).

Budget-raise: confirm-dialog 28.25 KB → 28.5 KB (measured 28.44 KB): ConfirmDialog explains a typed phrase that does not match on Enter (A6-157) and IconButton keeps a transparent disabled ghost fill (A6-232).

Budget-raise: locales-es 13.1 KB → 13.15 KB (measured 13.10 KB): The es catalog gained confirmDialog.phraseMismatch (A6-157).

Budget-raise: table-head-cell 5.45 KB → 5.5 KB (measured 5.45 KB): IconButton keeps a transparent fill while a ghost button is disabled (A6-232); every entry that renders IconButton carries the few extra bytes.

Budget-raise: toast-root 11.7 KB → 11.75 KB (measured 11.72 KB): Toast regions keep clear of the phone safe areas with env(safe-area-inset-*) (A6-008).

Budget-raise: sidebar-menu-sub-trigger 15.95 KB → 16 KB (measured 15.95 KB): IconButton keeps a transparent fill while a ghost button is disabled (A6-232); every entry that renders IconButton carries the few extra bytes.

Budget-raise: command 29.4 KB → 29.45 KB (measured 29.41 KB): Command shows its title in the phone sheet header (A6-003) and its dialog carries the useModalRoot and IconButton changes (A6-290, A6-232).

Budget-raise: drawer 23.45 KB → 23.5 KB (measured 23.45 KB): Drawer shares its root state through useModalRoot (A6-290) and IconButton keeps a transparent disabled ghost fill (A6-232).

Budget-raise: shortcuts-dialog 27.3 KB → 27.35 KB (measured 27.32 KB): ShortcutsDialog renders Dialog and IconButton, which carry the useModalRoot and disabled ghost changes (A6-290, A6-232).

Budget-raise: console-application-scene 127.2 KB → 127.25 KB (measured 127.21 KB): the scene renders Avatar and Eyebrow: the small Avatar presence dot now clears the second initial (A6-193) and the Eyebrow prefix stays beside the first word of a wrapped label (A6-095).

Budget-raise: logo-wall-section 25.45 KB → 25.5 KB (measured 25.46 KB): the band renders an Eyebrow, whose prefix now stays beside the first word of a wrapped label (A6-095).

Budget-raise: pricing-plans-section 22.25 KB → 22.3 KB (measured 22.27 KB): PricingCard hides its plan label when the price already names the plan (A6-210, owner call).

Budget-raise: pricing-card 5.4 KB → 5.5 KB (measured 5.45 KB): PricingCard hides its plan label when the price already names the plan (A6-210, owner call).

Budget-raise: media-scroll 5.75 KB → 5.85 KB (measured 5.84 KB): MediaScroll renders each claim title as a plain heading below lg and the button only from lg, through useMediaQuery, so phones get no dead tab stops (A6-096).

Budget-raise: console-showcase-section 22.3 KB → 22.35 KB (measured 22.31 KB): the view switcher lays out as an even grid on a phone instead of wrapping one view onto its own row (A6-098, A6-110).

Budget-raise: feature-scroll-section 21 KB → 21.1 KB (measured 21.07 KB): an odd last tile spans both tablet columns and a phone code sample drops its side inset (A6-115, A6-117).

Budget-raise: client-mosaic-section 8.3 KB → 8.8 KB (measured 8.79 KB): the band works out the tablet grid placement (lib/trailing-spans.ts) so a short last row of marks ends flush instead of leaving empty framed slots (A6-105).

Budget-raise: client-story-columns-section 9.25 KB → 9.5 KB (measured 9.49 KB): a face without an explicit ink takes dark ink when white falls under 4.5:1 (lib/face-ink.ts), and the default layout draws a link cue under each story (A6-107, A6-108).

Budget-raise: feature-tabs-section 60.6 KB → 60.65 KB (measured 60.62 KB): the band scene sits inside the pane's spacing-xl inset in its own wrapper (A6-119).

Budget-raise: closing-call-to-action-section 9.85 KB → 9.9 KB (measured 9.86 KB): CallToAction's panel copy takes a fixed measure so stacked bands start their actions at one place (A6-109).

## Audit-6 fixes, lane L04 (2026-11-25)

Budget-raise: data-table 58.45 KB → 58.6 KB (measured 58.54 KB): table rows repaint their bottom rule above the sideways-scroll fade so a frozen column leaves no notch, and plain cell text is dir=auto (A6-059, A6-061).

Budget-raise: console-application-scene 127.2 KB → 127.3 KB (measured 127.23 KB): the scene renders Table rows, which now repaint their bottom rule above the sideways-scroll fade (A6-059).

Budget-raise: console-trace-scene 86.4 KB → 87.05 KB (measured 87.01 KB): the scene renders LogViewer, whose header now hosts the copy control and a loading Skeleton, and Table rows that repaint their rule above the scroll fade (A6-049, A6-065, A6-059).

Budget-raise: listing-index-section 43.6 KB → 43.7 KB (measured 43.63 KB): the section renders Table rows, which now repaint their bottom rule above the sideways-scroll fade (A6-059).

Budget-raise: listing-table-section 26.7 KB → 26.75 KB (measured 26.71 KB): the section renders Table rows, which now repaint their bottom rule above the sideways-scroll fade (A6-059).

Budget-raise: chart 27.55 KB → 27.8 KB (measured 27.76 KB): ChartReference moves a band label off the band when a series crosses it, and legend buttons grow to 44 px on a coarse pointer (A6-056, A6-170).

Budget-raise: chart-legend 1.5 KB → 1.55 KB (measured 1.51 KB): legend buttons grow to 44 px on a coarse pointer (A6-170).

Budget-raise: chart-reference 2 KB → 2.15 KB (measured 2.11 KB): a band label checks the shown series at the last category and moves above or below the band when the line would cross it (A6-056); a tighter rewrite measured larger.

Budget-raise: tracker 6.4 KB → 6.6 KB (measured 6.54 KB): a touch tap opens the day readout and a capture listener closes it on a tap outside, and the error banner is a slot (A6-064, A6-060); onClickOutside measured 0.7 KB more.

Budget-raise: tree 11 KB → 11.1 KB (measured 11.03 KB): labels and sizes are dir=auto so they keep their order in right-to-left (A6-061).

Budget-raise: locales-es 13.1 KB → 13.15 KB (measured 13.10 KB): new flowDiagram.route string for the stacked FlowDiagram routes (A6-052).

Budget-raise: code-editor 11.6 KB → 11.65 KB (measured 11.60 KB): problem messages wrap instead of truncating (A6-058).

Budget-raise: log-viewer 44 KB → 44.45 KB (measured 44.41 KB): LogViewerHeader hosts the copy control and a loading Skeleton (A6-049, A6-065).

Budget-raise: log-viewer-header 5.15 KB → 18.1 KB (measured 18.06 KB): the header now renders the copy control (CopyButton, moved from LogViewerContent so the log body keeps its full width on a phone) and a loading Skeleton (A6-049, A6-057, A6-065); loading CopyButton as an async chunk added chunk overhead to every entry that renders LogViewer, so it stays static.

Budget-raise: table 9 KB → 9.05 KB (measured 9.01 KB): rows repaint their bottom rule above the sideways-scroll fade (A6-059).

Budget-raise: table-head-cell 5.45 KB → 5.5 KB (measured 5.46 KB): the sort button drops its generic aria-label when the header names it (A6-062).

Budget-raise: flow-diagram 22.1 KB → 22.25 KB (measured 22.20 KB): a stacked diagram lists the edges that skip a step as routes under their source card (A6-052).

Budget-raise: locales-es 13.1 KB → 13.15 KB (measured 13.13 KB): the FileTokenGroup catalog gained Cancel upload and the upload progress strings, so an uploading file names its cancel action and states its amount (audit-6 A6-037).

Budget-raise: multi-select 31.5 KB → 31.55 KB (measured 31.50 KB): MultiSelect renders Chip and Checkbox; a clickable Chip gained its 44px coarse-pointer hit area and a disabled checked Checkbox draws a readable mark (audit-6 A6-015, A6-033).

Budget-raise: tag-editor 27.3 KB → 27.35 KB (measured 27.31 KB): AttributeEditor names Remove (and TagEditor its Undo) by the row key instead of its position, and sets the limit text in the hint style (audit-6 A6-022, A6-030).

Budget-raise: call-to-action 3.7 KB → 3.75 KB (measured 3.70 KB): CallToAction starts its actions at one place (A6-109), and the Eyebrow prefix stays beside the first word when the label wraps (A6-095).

Budget-raise: console-application-scene 127.3 KB → 127.45 KB (measured 127.44 KB): The console scenes fade at the crop on a phone (A6-100), and the switches, checkboxes, settings rows, item descriptions and table rows the scene draws carry their own fixes (A6-033, A6-138, A6-145, A6-059).

Budget-raise: console-trace-scene 87.05 KB → 87.15 KB (measured 87.11 KB): The console scenes fade at the crop on a phone (A6-100), a stacked FlowDiagram keeps every edge and its severity (A6-052), the Trace minimap handles take a 44 px tap (A6-063) and the LogViewer copy moves into its header toolbar (A6-049).

Budget-raise: article-body-section 49.5 KB → 49.55 KB (measured 49.53 KB): The breadcrumb folds only when its segments do not fit (A6-066), a menu shortcut reads with the platform modifier (A6-073), DocProse keeps inline code in a table cell on one line (A6-258), and the Eyebrow prefix stays beside the first word when the label wraps (A6-095).

Budget-raise: network-section 28.15 KB → 28.2 KB (measured 28.16 KB): NetworkSection wraps its claims eyebrow before the map (A6-128), and the Eyebrow prefix stays beside the first word when the label wraps (A6-095).

Budget-raise: client-quotes-section 28.55 KB → 28.6 KB (measured 28.56 KB): The Eyebrow prefix stays beside the first word when the label wraps (A6-095), and the Avatar presence dot clears the second initial (A6-193).

Budget-raise: job-posting-section 63.7 KB → 63.8 KB (measured 63.79 KB): PhoneInput lists countries A to Z with no national mask as placeholder (A6-016, A6-017), an uploading file token offers Cancel upload and states its progress (A6-037), controls inside an InputGroup match its height (A6-018), and the Eyebrow prefix stays beside the first word when the label wraps (A6-095).

Budget-raise: donut-chart 13.9 KB → 13.95 KB (measured 13.90 KB): Chart legend items are 44 px tall on a touch screen (A6-070), and IconButton keeps a transparent disabled ghost fill (A6-232).

Budget-raise: timeline 22.7 KB → 22.75 KB (measured 22.73 KB): Timestamp gains the time format that Timeline items inside a dated group print (A6-042).

Budget-raise: config-provider 14.55 KB → 14.6 KB (measured 14.55 KB): The en catalog it bundles gained the Try again, cancel-upload, upload-progress, phrase-mismatch and route messages (A6-039, A6-037, A6-157, A6-052).

Budget-raise: locales-pt-BR 13 KB → 13.05 KB (measured 13.02 KB): The pt-BR catalog gained the Try again, cancel-upload, upload-progress, phrase-mismatch and route messages (A6-039, A6-037, A6-157, A6-052).

Budget-raise: prompt-input 16 KB → 16.05 KB (measured 16.00 KB): The empty composer keeps a readable text field on a phone (A6-081), the model menu drops keyboard shortcuts on touch (A6-230), and a clickable chip grows to a 44 px hit area on touch (A6-015).

Budget-raise: attribute-editor 25.4 KB → 25.45 KB (measured 25.43 KB): AttributeEditor names Remove by the row key (A6-022), and a selected Field choice card takes the RadioGroup card fill in the field label it renders (A6-029).

Budget-raise: button-group 17.8 KB → 17.85 KB (measured 17.80 KB): Grouped icon buttons reach the 44 px touch width (A6-005), and a menu item prints its shortcut with the platform modifier (A6-073).

Budget-raise: image-gallery 27.75 KB → 27.8 KB (measured 27.75 KB): ImageGallery shows Try again and emits retry when an image fails (A6-039), and its Dialog shares its root state through useModalRoot (A6-290).

Budget-raise: avatar 12.8 KB → 12.85 KB (measured 12.80 KB): An avatar menu trigger is named by its aria-label and has a touch target (A6-069), and the small presence dot clears the second initial (A6-193).

Budget-raise: log-viewer-steps 42.4 KB → 42.45 KB (measured 42.40 KB): The LogViewer copy sits in the header toolbar and the header is honest while loading (A6-049, A6-057).

Budget-raise: table-sort-button 3.8 KB → 3.85 KB (measured 3.80 KB): A sort button named by its header carries no generic aria-label (A6-062), and IconButton keeps a transparent disabled ghost fill (A6-232).

Budget-raise: dialog 23.3 KB → 23.35 KB (measured 23.31 KB): Dialog shares its root state through useModalRoot (A6-290), and the en catalog it bundles through ConfigProvider gained the Try again, cancel-upload, upload-progress, phrase-mismatch and route messages (A6-039, A6-037, A6-157, A6-052).

Budget-raise: css-button 11.05 KB → 11.1 KB (measured 11.09 KB gzip): the theme stylesheet every app compiles gains the brand-tint text selection (A6-026), the open word spacing of display headings on phones (A6-122) and the 16px code step with a real line height for wrapping code (A6-180).

Budget-raise: css-icons 15.35 KB → 15.45 KB (measured 15.41 KB gzip): the same theme additions as css-button (A6-026, A6-122, A6-180).

Budget-raise: css-console 24.7 KB → 24.95 KB (measured 24.91 KB gzip): the same theme additions (A6-026, A6-122, A6-180), plus the pointer-coarse 44px touch targets of grouped icon buttons, chips and chart legend items (A6-005, A6-015, A6-070), the transparent disabled ghost and outline IconButton fill (A6-232), the whole table row rules beside a frozen column (A6-059) and the platform shortcut hint in menu items (A6-073).

### Audit-6 wave 2, lead merge

Budget-raise: stats 2.3 KB → 2.35 KB (measured 2.33 KB): the A6-099 unit role (text-big-number-unit) on top of the A6-W6 entry measure.

Budget-raise: feature-scroll-section 21.15 KB → 21.2 KB (measured 21.15 KB): CodeBlock tab hit area and narrow inset (A6-048, A6-117).

Budget-raise: cli-terminal-section 48.05 KB → 48.2 KB (measured 48.17 KB): CodeBlock tab hit area and narrow inset (A6-048, A6-117).

Budget-raise: feature-tabs-section 60.75 KB → 60.85 KB (measured 60.83 KB): CodeBlock tab hit area and narrow inset (A6-048, A6-117) and the A6-118 example labels.

Budget-raise: contact-hub-section 9.85 KB → 9.9 KB (measured 9.86 KB): the channel severity field (A6-111).

Budget-raise: code-block 34.45 KB → 34.5 KB (measured 34.45 KB): the file tab hit area and the narrow-block inset (A6-048, A6-117).

Budget-raise: streaming-response 43.95 KB → 44 KB (measured 43.96 KB): the AI chat inline code chips (A6-229) and the CodeBlock tab hit area (A6-048).

Budget-raise: copy-text 10.8 KB → 10.85 KB (measured 10.81 KB): the wrapLines prop (CopyText wrap handoff).

Budget-raise: chat-message 20.6 KB → 20.8 KB (measured 20.79 KB): inside a Conversation, ChatMessage.Actions renders its row only when it nears the log's view, through one IntersectionObserver (A6-270); on the long-thread block it cuts the mounted nodes on a phone from 895 to 541 and the blocking time by about a third. Shrunk first: a direct observer in place of VueUse's helper saved 0.2 KB.

Budget-raise: chat-message-actions 14.5 KB → 14.7 KB (measured 14.68 KB): the same lazy action row inside a Conversation (A6-270); a direct IntersectionObserver in place of VueUse's helper kept the growth to 0.2 KB.
